`packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/Roll.ts`:

```ts
// World War 1936 P3 (spec §22.2): the only randomness of politics. Stateless:
// the same inputs give the same value whatever ran before, so a restored game
// and a game with more content roll the same.
import type { AiWeight } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/PoliticsTypes";

/** splitmix32 finaliser. */
function mix32(x: number): number {
  let z = (x + 0x9e3779b9) | 0;
  z = Math.imul(z ^ (z >>> 16), 0x85ebca6b);
  z = Math.imul(z ^ (z >>> 13), 0xc2b2ae35);
  return (z ^ (z >>> 16)) >>> 0;
}

/** A 32-bit unsigned value from (seed, purpose, a, b, c). */
export function wwRoll(seed: number, purpose: number, a: number, b = 0, c = 0): number {
  let h = mix32(seed | 0);
  h = mix32((h ^ purpose) | 0);
  h = mix32((h ^ a) | 0);
  h = mix32((h ^ b) | 0);
  return mix32((h ^ c) | 0);
}

/** FNV-1a of an id string (content ids, purposes). */
export function idHash(id: string): number {
  let h = 0x811c9dc5;
  for (let i = 0; i < id.length; i++) {
    h ^= id.charCodeAt(i);
    h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193);
  }
  return h >>> 0;
}

export const P_BEAT = idHash("beat");
export const P_BRANCH = idHash("branch");
export const P_EVT = idHash("evt");
export const P_MTTH = idHash("mtth");
export const P_FOCUS = idHash("focus");
export const P_RAND = idHash("rand");
export const P_ELECTION = idHash("election");
export const P_DELAY = idHash("delay");

/**
 * Spec §12.4, §18.5: weight = base × Π factor/100 (floor each step) over
 * the mods whose condition holds. `holds` evaluates a mod's condition.
 */
export function aiWeight(w: AiWeight | undefined, holds: (i: number) => boolean, base = 10): number {
  let v = w?.base ?? base;
  (w?.mods ?? []).forEach((m, i) => {
    if (holds(i)) v = Math.floor((v * m.factor) / 100);
  });
  return Math.max(0, v);
}

/** Index picked by `roll` over the weights; -1 when every weight is 0. */
export function pickWeighted(weights: readonly number[], roll: number): number {
  let total = 0;
  for (const w of weights) total += w;
  if (total <= 0) return -1;
  let r = roll % total;
  for (let i = 0; i < weights.length; i++) {
    if (r < weights[i]) return i;
    r -= weights[i];
  }
  return weights.length - 1;
}
```

`packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/Stats.ts`:

```ts
// World War 1936 P3 (spec §3): every modifier is one WwStats; sources add up.
import { TechBranch } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/EconomyTypes";
import { WW_STAT_KEYS, WwStatKey, WwStats } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/PoliticsTypes";
import type { TechTotals } from "./WwTechs";

export function emptyStats(): WwStats {
  const s = {} as WwStats;
  for (const k of WW_STAT_KEYS) s[k] = 0;
  return s;
}

/** Adds `p` into `out` (WW_STAT_KEYS order; missing keys are 0). */
export function addStatsInto(out: WwStats, p: Partial<WwStats>, times = 1): void {
  for (const k of WW_STAT_KEYS) {
    const v = p[k];
    if (v !== undefined) out[k] += v * times;
  }
}

export function sumStats(parts: readonly Partial<WwStats>[]): WwStats {
  const out = emptyStats();
  for (const p of parts) addStatsInto(out, p);
  return out;
}

/** The tech totals as stats (the P1+P2 keys are the first 34). */
export function techStats(t: TechTotals): WwStats {
  return sumStats([t as unknown as Partial<WwStats>]);
}

/** Spec §3.3: every …Pct sum is kept in −90..+300. */
export function clampStats(s: WwStats): WwStats {
  for (const k of WW_STAT_KEYS) {
    if (k.endsWith("Pct")) s[k] = Math.max(-90, Math.min(300, s[k]));
  }
  return s;
}

export const RS_STAT_OF_BRANCH: Readonly<Record<TechBranch, WwStatKey>> = {
  infantry: "rsInfantryPct", artillery: "rsArtilleryPct", armour: "rsArmorPct",
  air: "rsAirPct", industry: "rsIndustryPct", electronics: "rsElectronicsPct",
  doctrine: "rsDoctrinePct",
};

/** Spec §3.4: research speed % of a branch (the law's share is added by P1). */
export function researchSpeedPct(s: WwStats, branch: TechBranch): number {
  return s.researchPct + s[RS_STAT_OF_BRANCH[branch]];
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 정치 순수 규칙 `packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/PoliticsRules.ts`** (spec §4~§7, §14, §16; 화면과 엔진이 같이 쓴다. 결정 11)

```ts
import { LawValue, WwOrderBlock } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/EconomyTypes";
import {
  IDEO_COM, IDEO_DEM, IDEO_FAS, IDEO_NEU, POP_TOTAL, TensionAct, WW_PP_BASE_MILLI,
  WW_PP_CAP, WarGoalType, WwStats,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/PoliticsTypes";

// ---- Political power (spec §4.1) ----

export function dailyPpMilli(s: WwStats): number {
  return Math.floor((WW_PP_BASE_MILLI * (100 + s.ppGainPct)) / 100) + s.ppMilli;
}
/** Adds a day's gain; what goes over the cap is lost (spec §4.1). */
export function addPpCapped(ppMilli: number, gain: number): number {
  const cap = WW_PP_CAP * 1000;
  if (ppMilli >= cap) return ppMilli;
  return Math.min(cap, ppMilli + gain);
}
/** Spec §4.3: floor(base × (100 + pct) / 100), at least 0. PP, not milli. */
export function costWith(base: number, pct: number): number {
  return Math.max(0, Math.floor((base * (100 + pct)) / 100));
}

// ---- Popularity (spec §5.2) ----

/**
 * Adds `delta` bp to ideology `i` and takes it from (or gives it to) the
 * others in proportion; the integer remainder goes to the largest other
 * (ties: lower index), which stops at 0. The sum stays exactly 10,000.
 */
export function addPopularity(pop: number[], i: number, delta: number): void {
  const d = Math.max(-pop[i], Math.min(POP_TOTAL - pop[i], Math.trunc(delta)));
  if (d === 0) return;
  let others = 0;
  for (let j = 0; j < 4; j++) if (j !== i) others += pop[j];
  if (others === 0) {
    // Only when d < 0 (i held everything): the whole loss goes to one ideology.
    pop[i === IDEO_NEU ? IDEO_DEM : IDEO_NEU] -= d;
    pop[i] += d;
    return;
  }
  let moved = 0;
  for (let j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
    if (j === i) continue;
    const share = -Math.trunc((d * pop[j]) / others);
    pop[j] += share;
    moved += share;
  }
  let rest = -d - moved;
  while (rest !== 0) {
    let best = -1;
    for (let j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
      if (j === i || (rest < 0 && pop[j] === 0)) continue;
      if (best < 0 || pop[j] > pop[best]) best = j;
    }
    if (best < 0) break;
    const step = rest < 0 ? Math.max(rest, -pop[best]) : rest;
    pop[best] += step;
    rest -= step;
  }
  pop[i] += d;
}

/** Largest ideology other than `ruling` (ties: lower index). */
export function largestOther(pop: readonly number[], ruling: number): number {
  let best = -1;
  for (let j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
    if (j !== ruling && (best < 0 || pop[j] > pop[best])) best = j;
  }
  return best;
}

// ---- Stability and war support (spec §6) ----

/** Ruling-party line: (support − 50%) / 5, bp. */
export function rulingPartyBp(rulingPop: number): number {
  return Math.trunc((rulingPop - 5000) / 5);
}

/** Spec §6.2: the dynamic stats a stability value gives. */
export function stabilityEffects(s: number): Partial<WwStats> {
  if (s < 5000) {
    const out = -Math.floor(((5000 - s) * 4) / 1000);
    return { ppGainPct: -Math.floor(((5000 - s) * 5) / 1000), outputPct: out, constructionPct: out };
  }
  return { ppGainPct: Math.floor(((s - 5000) * 3) / 1000), outputPct: Math.floor(((s - 5000) * 2) / 1000) };
}

/** Spec §6.3. */
export function warSupportEffects(w: number): Partial<WwStats> {
  if (w < 5000) {
    return {
      recoveryPct: -Math.floor(((5000 - w) * 5) / 1000),
      recruitablePct: -Math.floor(((5000 - w) * 2) / 1000),
    };
  }
  return { recoveryPct: Math.floor(((w - 5000) * 2) / 1000) };
}

/** Spec §6.4. */
export function effectiveSurrenderPct(basePct: number, stabBp: number, wsBp: number, surrenderPp: number): number {
  const ws = Math.max(-10, Math.min(10, Math.floor((wsBp - 5000) / 500)));
  const st = Math.max(-5, Math.min(5, Math.floor((stabBp - 5000) / 1000)));
  return Math.max(50, Math.min(98, basePct + ws + st + surrenderPp));
}

/** Spec §6.1: war-weariness line, bp (≤ 0). */
export function wearinessBp(casualties: number, corePop: number, lostLegalVpPct: number): number {
  const cas = corePop <= 0 ? 0 : Math.min(3000, Math.floor((casualties * 400 * 100) / corePop));
  return -cas - lostLegalVpPct * 20;
}

// ---- Laws (spec §7.2) ----

export const LAW_PP: Readonly<Record<LawValue, number>> = {
  civilian: 50, early_mobilization: 75, partial_mobilization: 100, war_economy: 150,
  total_mobilization: 150, free_trade: 100, export_focus: 75, limited_exports: 75,
  closed_economy: 100, disarmed: 50, volunteer: 50, limited: 50, extensive: 100, service: 125,
  all_adults: 150, scraping: 150,
};
const CONSCRIPTION_VALUES: readonly LawValue[] = [
  "disarmed", "volunteer", "limited", "extensive", "service", "all_adults", "scraping",
];
/** Spec §7.2 stability lines of the laws in force, bp. */
export const LAW_STABILITY_BP: Readonly<Partial<Record<LawValue, number>>> = {
  war_economy: -300, total_mobilization: -800, all_adults: -500, scraping: -1000,
};

export interface LawCtx {
  wsBp: number;
  wtBp: number;
  ruling: number;
  atWar: boolean;
  ppMilli: number;
  costMilli: number;
}

/** Spec §7.2: why `value` cannot be set now; null if it can (cooldown and "same" are P1's). */
export function lawBlock(value: LawValue, ctx: LawCtx): WwOrderBlock | null {
  const dem = ctx.ruling === IDEO_DEM;
  const gate = (ws: number, wt: number, needWar: boolean): WwOrderBlock | null => {
    if (needWar && !ctx.atWar) return "law_condition";
    if (ctx.wsBp < ws) return "law_ws";
    if (dem && !ctx.atWar && ctx.wtBp < wt) return "law_tension";
    return null;
  };
  let block: WwOrderBlock | null = null;
  switch (value) {
    case "partial_mobilization":
      block = ctx.atWar ? null : gate(3000, 2500, false);
      break;
    case "war_economy":
      block = ctx.atWar ? null : gate(5000, 5000, false);
      break;
    case "total_mobilization":
      block = gate(7000, 0, true);
      break;
    case "extensive":
      block = gate(3000, 2500, false);
      break;
    case "service":
      block = gate(5000, 5000, false);
      break;
    case "all_adults":
      block = gate(7000, 0, true);
      break;
    case "scraping":
      block = gate(8000, 0, true);
      break;
    case "free_trade":
      block = ctx.ruling === IDEO_COM || ctx.ruling === IDEO_FAS ? "law_ideology" : null;
      break;
    case "closed_economy":
      block = ctx.ruling === IDEO_COM || ctx.ruling === IDEO_FAS || ctx.atWar ? null : "law_ideology";
      break;
    default:
      block = null;
  }
  if (block !== null) return block;
  return ctx.ppMilli < 0 || ctx.costMilli > ctx.ppMilli ? "no_pp" : null;
}

/** Spec §7.1: the target law's cost in PP. */
export function lawCost(value: LawValue, s: WwStats): number {
  const pct = s.lawCostPct + (CONSCRIPTION_VALUES.includes(value) ? s.conscriptionCostPct : 0);
  return costWith(LAW_PP[value], pct);
}

// ---- War goals, justification, tension (spec §14) ----

export function justifyCost(targetMajor: boolean, s: WwStats): number {
  return costWith(targetMajor ? 75 : 50, s.justifyCostPct);
}

/** Spec §14.3: days, at least 10. */
export function justifyDays(s: WwStats, targetEnemyJustifyPct: number, majorGuarantors: number): number {
  const pct = 100 + s.justifyTimePct + targetEnemyJustifyPct + majorGuarantors * 25;
  return Math.max(10, Math.floor((100 * pct) / 100));
}

/** Spec §14.1: tension an act adds, bp, with the actor's tensionPct. */
export function tensionFor(act: TensionAct, targetMajor: boolean, s: WwStats, goal?: WarGoalType): number {
  let pct: number;
  switch (act) {
    case "justified": pct = targetMajor ? 4 : 2; break;
    case "declare_goal": pct = targetMajor ? 5 : 2; break;
    case "declare_no_goal": pct = 2 * (targetMajor ? 5 : 2) + 4; break;
    case "capitulation": pct = 2; break;
  }
  let bp = pct * 100;
  if (goal === "claim" && act !== "capitulation") bp = Math.floor(bp / 2);
  return Math.max(0, Math.floor((bp * (100 + s.tensionPct)) / 100));
}

export interface JustifyGateCtx {
  ruling: number;
  wtBp: number;
  targetThreatBp: number;
  /** The target is in a faction at war with me or my faction. */
  targetInEnemyFaction: boolean;
  atWarWith: boolean;
  sameFaction: boolean;
  nap: boolean;
  iGuarantee: boolean;
  noJustifyRule: boolean;
  busy: boolean;
}
/** Spec §14.3 conditions: why I cannot start justifying on the target. */
export function justifyGate(c: JustifyGateCtx): WwOrderBlock | null {
  if (c.busy) return "justify_busy";
  if (c.atWarWith || c.sameFaction || c.nap || c.iGuarantee || c.noJustifyRule) return "justify_blocked";
  if (c.ruling === IDEO_DEM) {
    const ok = (c.wtBp >= 5000 && c.targetThreatBp >= 500) || c.wtBp >= 8000 || c.targetInEnemyFaction;
    return ok ? null : "tension_gate";
  }
  if (c.ruling === IDEO_NEU) return c.wtBp >= 2500 ? null : "tension_gate";
  return null;
}

/** Spec §14.4, §16.1: may a government guarantee now? */
export function guaranteeGate(ruling: number, wtBp: number, targetJustifiedAgainst: boolean, gatePct = 25): boolean {
  if (ruling !== IDEO_DEM) return true;
  return targetJustifiedAgainst || wtBp >= gatePct * 100;
}

/** Spec §14.4, §15.3: may a government create / join / invite into a faction now? */
export function factionGate(ruling: number, wtBp: number, minusPp = 0): boolean {
  if (ruling !== IDEO_DEM && ruling !== IDEO_NEU) return true;
  return wtBp >= Math.max(0, 40 - minusPp) * 100;
}
```

- spec §7.2의 "전쟁지지도 ≥ N% **또는 전쟁 중**"(부분 동원·전시 경제)은 `case`의 `ctx.atWar ? null`이, "전쟁 중 **그리고**"(총동원·전 성인·마지막 동원)는 `needWar`가 맡는다. 확장 징병·의무 복무는 전쟁 중에도 전쟁지지도를 본다(표 그대로). 긴장 조건은 전쟁 중이면 사라진다.
- `PoliticsRules.test.ts`가 §7.2 표의 모든 칸(법 16개 × {평시·전쟁} × {민주·비민주} × 문턱 바로 아래/위)을 `it.each`로 본다.

- [ ] **Step 6: 설명 글 `packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/Describe.ts`** (spec §20.4, 결정 25. 화면이 쓰고 엔진의 막힌 조건 번호도 이 순서다)

```ts
import type { Condition, Effect, LeafCondition, RegionPick, Selector } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/PoliticsDsl";
import { WW_STAT_KEYS, WwStats } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/PoliticsTypes";

export interface DescLine {
  /** ww1936.fx.<kind>, ww1936.cond.<kind>, ww1936.stat.<key>, or a header (…all, …any, …not, …if, …else, …random_option). */
  key: string;
  params: Record<string, string | number>;
  depth: number;
  /** For conditions: the leaf number (conditionLeaves order); -1 for headers and effects. */
  leaf: number;
}

/** "@self", "@from", "@target", a tag, "@leader:<inner>", "@owner:<group>". */
export function selectorCode(s: Selector | undefined): string {
  if (s === undefined || s === "self") return "@self";
  if (s === "from" || s === "target") return `@${s}`;
  if ("tag" in s) return s.tag;
  if ("factionLeaderOf" in s) return `@leader:${selectorCode(s.factionLeaderOf)}`;
  return `@owner:${s.ownerOf}`;
}
export function pickCode(p: RegionPick): string {
  if (p === "capital") return "capital";
  if ("group" in p) return `group:${p.group}`;
  if ("best" in p) return `best:${p.best}${p.of ? `:${p.of}` : ""}`;
  if ("coreBest" in p) return `core:${p.coreBest}`;
  if ("borderWith" in p) return `border:${p.borderWith === "threat" ? "threat" : selectorCode(p.borderWith)}:${p.n}`;
  if ("acquired" in p) return `acquired:${p.n}`;
  return `coastal:${p.n}`;
}

/** Primitive fields as params; selectors and picks as codes; nested lists are lines of their own. */
function paramsOf(o: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, string | number> {
  const out: Record<string, string | number> = {};
  const picks = o.kind === "move_buildings" ? ["where", "from", "to"] : ["where"];
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(o)) {
    if (k === "kind" || k === "then" || k === "else" || k === "options" || k === "cond") continue;
    if (typeof v === "number" || typeof v === "string") out[k] = v;
    else if (typeof v === "boolean") out[k] = v ? 1 : 0;
    else if (picks.includes(k)) out[k] = pickCode(v as RegionPick);
    else if (v !== undefined) out[k] = selectorCode(v as Selector);
  }
  if (!("who" in out)) out.who = "@self";
  return out;
}

export function describeEffects(effects: readonly Effect[], depth = 0): DescLine[] {
  const out: DescLine[] = [];
  for (const e of effects) {
    out.push({ key: `ww1936.fx.${e.kind}`, params: paramsOf(e as Record<string, unknown>), depth, leaf: -1 });
    if (e.kind === "if") {
      out.push(...describeCondition(e.cond, depth + 1).map((l) => ({ ...l, leaf: -1 })));
      out.push(...describeEffects(e.then, depth + 1));
      if (e.else !== undefined) {
        out.push({ key: "ww1936.fx.else", params: {}, depth, leaf: -1 });
        out.push(...describeEffects(e.else, depth + 1));
      }
    } else if (e.kind === "random_list") {
      for (const o of e.options) {
        out.push({ key: "ww1936.fx.random_option", params: { weight: o.weight }, depth: depth + 1, leaf: -1 });
        out.push(...describeEffects(o.effects, depth + 2));
      }
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/** Leaves in depth-first order; their index is the "leaf number". */
export function conditionLeaves(c: Condition | undefined): LeafCondition[] {
  if (c === undefined) return [];
  if ("all" in c) return c.all.flatMap(conditionLeaves);
  if ("any" in c) return c.any.flatMap(conditionLeaves);
  if ("not" in c) return conditionLeaves(c.not);
  return [c];
}

export function describeCondition(c: Condition | undefined, depth = 0): DescLine[] {
  const out: DescLine[] = [];
  let n = 0;
  const walk = (x: Condition, d: number): void => {
    if ("all" in x || "any" in x) {
      const list = "all" in x ? x.all : x.any;
      if (list.length > 1) out.push({ key: `ww1936.cond.${"all" in x ? "all" : "any"}`, params: {}, depth: d, leaf: -1 });
      for (const y of list) walk(y, list.length > 1 ? d + 1 : d);
    } else if ("not" in x) {
      out.push({ key: "ww1936.cond.not", params: {}, depth: d, leaf: -1 });
      walk(x.not, d + 1);
    } else {
      out.push({ key: `ww1936.cond.${x.kind}`, params: paramsOf(x as Record<string, unknown>), depth: d, leaf: n++ });
    }
  };
  if (c !== undefined) walk(c, depth);
  return out;
}

/** One line per non-zero stat, WW_STAT_KEYS order. */
export function describeStats(s: Partial<WwStats>): DescLine[] {
  const out: DescLine[] = [];
  for (const k of WW_STAT_KEYS) {
    const v = s[k];
    if (v !== undefined && v !== 0) out.push({ key: `ww1936.stat.${k}`, params: { value: v }, depth: 0, leaf: -1 });
  }
  return out;
}
```

- 주 고르기(`RegionPick`)는 `where`와 `move_buildings`의 `from`·`to`뿐이고, 나머지 객체 필드는 모두 `Selector`다(`"self"` 같은 문자열 선택자는 그대로 문자열 매개변수가 된다 — 화면이 `@` 없는 값도 선택자로 읽는다). 테스트(`Dsl.test.ts` "every kind describes to its key")가 `EFFECT_KINDS`·`CONDITION_KINDS` 견본마다 첫 줄의 `key`가 `ww1936.fx.<kind>`/`ww1936.cond.<kind>`인지, `conditionLeaves(c).length`가 `describeCondition(c)`의 `leaf ≥ 0` 줄 수와 같은지 본다.

- [ ] **Step 7: 콘텐츠 형식과 등록 (`packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/content/`)** (spec §20, 결정 6, 7, 31)

갈래끼리 같은 파일을 고치지 않도록 **여럿이 더하는 표는 갈래마다 파일 하나**로 나눈다(spec §20.1의 "접두사 구역"을 파일로 바꾼 것 — 결정 31):

```
content/
  types.ts            기반: 타입 재수출(FocusDef…TemplateUnlockDef, Condition, Effect)
  helpers.ts          기반: 자료를 짧게 쓰는 도우미(유일하게 함수가 있는 파일)
  registry.ts         기반: buildRegistry, CONTENT
  advisors.ts         기반: TRAITS(특성 36개 전부)
  treaties.ts         기반: START_NAPS(spec §16.2의 6개)
  history.ts          기반: HISTORY_BEATS = P0 48줄(safety) + "// ---- new beats (K5) ----" 아래 K5
  focus/generic.ts    K1: GENERIC_FOCUSES, GENERIC_PATH, GENERIC_BRANCHES
  focus/deu.ts        K2: DEU_FOCUSES, DEU_PATH, DEU_BRANCHES       (gbr.ts K3, sun.ts K4: 같은 모양)
  events/{generic,deu,gbr,sun,world}.ts   K1, K2, K3, K4, K5: <X>_EVENTS
  decisions/generic.ts K1: GENERIC_DECISIONS; decisions/national.ts K5: NATIONAL_DECISIONS
  spirits/{gen,deu,gbr,sun,world,start}.ts K1, K2, K3, K4, K5, K6: SPIRITS_<X>
  persons/{deu,gbr,sun,leaders}.ts         K2, K3, K4, K6: PERSONS_<X>
  templates/{deu,gbr,sun,gen}.ts           K2, K3, K4, K1: TEMPLATES_<X>
  flags/{base,gen,deu,gbr,sun,world}.ts    기반, K1, K2, K3, K4, K5: FLAGS_<X>
  countries.ts        K6: COUNTRY_POLITICS, CLAIMS, FACTION_NAMES
  regionGroups.ts     K5: REGION_GROUPS
```

기반은 갈래 파일을 **빈 배열**(`export const DEU_FOCUSES: FocusDef[] = [];` 등)로 만든다. `flags/base.ts`에는 spec이 이미 이름을 정한 깃발을 모두 적는다: `munich_done`, `mr_secret_protocol`, `pact_of_steel`, `tripartite`, `abdication_resolved`, `edward_viii`, `kings_party`, `purge_path`, `winter_war_fought`, `coup_failed`, `can_create_faction`, `lend_lease_ready`, `lend_lease`, `northern_road_closed`, `sudeten_transferred`, `anschluss_done`, `prague_done`, `memel_done`, `baltics_annexed`, `bessarabia_lost`, `elections_suspended`.

`content/helpers.ts`(도우미 — 자료 파일이 import한다):

```ts
import type { CountryTag } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import type { Condition, Effect, Selector } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/PoliticsDsl";
import type { FocusId } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/PoliticsTypes";

export const T = (tag: CountryTag): Selector => ({ tag });
export const all = (...c: Condition[]): Condition => ({ all: c });
export const any = (...c: Condition[]): Condition => ({ any: c });
export const not = (c: Condition): Condition => ({ not: c });
export const after = (date: string): Condition => ({ kind: "date_ge", date });
export const before = (date: string): Condition => ({ kind: "date_lt", date });
export const done = (id: FocusId): Condition => ({ kind: "focus_done", id });
export const flag = (f: string): Condition => ({ kind: "flag", flag: f });
export const gflag = (f: string): Condition => ({ kind: "gflag", flag: f });
export const exists = (tag: CountryTag): Condition => ({ kind: "exists", who: { tag } });
/** One prerequisite group list from "a|b, c" shorthand is not allowed: write [[a, b], [c]]. */
export const fire = (id: string, to?: Selector, days?: number): Effect =>
  ({ kind: "fire_event", id, ...(to ? { to } : {}), ...(days ? { days } : {}) });
```

`content/registry.ts`:

```ts
import type { CountryTag } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import {
  BranchPoint, ClaimDef, CountryPoliticsDef, DecisionDef, EventDef, FactionNameDef, FOCUS_TREES,
  FocusDef, FocusId, FocusTreeId, HistoryBeat, PersonDef, RegionGroupDef, SpiritDef,
  TemplateUnlockDef, TraitDef, TraitId,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/PoliticsTypes";
import { dayOfDate } from "../Calendar";
import { idHash } from "../Roll";
// …every lane file (see the list above)…

export interface ContentParts {
  focuses: readonly FocusDef[];
  /** By tree; the generic path may hold "@ideo" and "@ideo_ally" (Task 2 expands them). */
  paths: Partial<Record<FocusTreeId, readonly FocusId[]>>;
  branchPoints: Partial<Record<FocusTreeId, readonly BranchPoint[]>>;
  events: readonly EventDef[];
  decisions: readonly DecisionDef[];
  spirits: readonly SpiritDef[];
  traits: readonly TraitDef[];
  persons: readonly PersonDef[];
  beats: readonly HistoryBeat[];
  groups: readonly RegionGroupDef[];
  countries: readonly CountryPoliticsDef[];
  claims: readonly ClaimDef[];
  factionNames: readonly FactionNameDef[];
  templates: readonly TemplateUnlockDef[];
  flags: readonly string[];
  startNaps: readonly (readonly [CountryTag, CountryTag])[];
}

export interface ContentRegistry extends ContentParts {
  focusIdx: Map<FocusId, number>;
  /** Focus indices of each tree in content order; a focus's place in this list is its tree position. */
  treeFocuses: Record<FocusTreeId, number[]>;
  treePos: Map<FocusId, number>;
  eventIdx: Map<string, number>;
  eventHash: number[];
  eventsByTag: Map<CountryTag, number[]>;
  eventsAny: number[];
  /** day → event indices with a date, in event order. */
  dateEvents: Map<number, number[]>;
  decisionIdx: Map<string, number>;
  decisionsFor: Map<CountryTag, number[]>;
  decisionsAny: number[];
  spiritIdx: Map<string, SpiritDef>;
  traitIdx: Map<TraitId, TraitDef>;
  personIdx: Map<string, PersonDef>;
  personsOf: Map<CountryTag, PersonDef[]>;
  groupIdx: Map<string, RegionGroupDef>;
  countryIdx: Map<CountryTag, CountryPoliticsDef>;
  beatHash: number[];
  flagSet: Set<string>;
}

const push = <K, V>(m: Map<K, V[]>, k: K, v: V) => {
  const l = m.get(k);
  if (l === undefined) m.set(k, [v]);
  else l.push(v);
};

export function buildRegistry(p: ContentParts): ContentRegistry {
  const treeFocuses = Object.fromEntries(FOCUS_TREES.map((t) => [t, [] as number[]])) as Record<FocusTreeId, number[]>;
  const focusIdx = new Map<FocusId, number>();
  const treePos = new Map<FocusId, number>();
  p.focuses.forEach((f, i) => {
    focusIdx.set(f.id, i);
    treePos.set(f.id, treeFocuses[f.tree].length);
    treeFocuses[f.tree].push(i);
  });
  const eventIdx = new Map<string, number>();
  const eventsByTag = new Map<CountryTag, number[]>();
  const eventsAny: number[] = [];
  const dateEvents = new Map<number, number[]>();
  p.events.forEach((e, i) => {
    eventIdx.set(e.id, i);
    if (e.date !== undefined) push(dateEvents, dayOfDate(e.date.on), i);
    if (e.trigger === undefined) return;
    if (e.receivers === "any") eventsAny.push(i);
    else for (const t of e.receivers ?? []) push(eventsByTag, t, i);
  });
  const decisionIdx = new Map<string, number>();
  const decisionsFor = new Map<CountryTag, number[]>();
  const decisionsAny: number[] = [];
  p.decisions.forEach((d, i) => {
    decisionIdx.set(d.id, i);
    if (d.for === "any") decisionsAny.push(i);
    else for (const t of d.for) push(decisionsFor, t, i);
  });
  const personsOf = new Map<CountryTag, PersonDef[]>();
  for (const x of p.persons) push(personsOf, x.country, x);
  return {
    ...p,
    focusIdx, treeFocuses, treePos, eventIdx, eventHash: p.events.map((e) => idHash(e.id)),
    eventsByTag, eventsAny, dateEvents, decisionIdx, decisionsFor, decisionsAny,
    spiritIdx: new Map(p.spirits.map((s) => [s.id, s])),
    traitIdx: new Map(p.traits.map((t) => [t.id, t])),
    personIdx: new Map(p.persons.map((x) => [x.id, x])),
    personsOf,
    groupIdx: new Map(p.groups.map((g) => [g.id, g])),
    countryIdx: new Map(p.countries.map((c) => [c.tag, c])),
    beatHash: p.beats.map((b) => idHash(b.id)),
    flagSet: new Set(p.flags),
  };
}

/** Everything in its fixed file order (event numbers follow this order, spec §12.3). */
export const CONTENT_PARTS: ContentParts = {
  focuses: [...GENERIC_FOCUSES, ...DEU_FOCUSES, ...GBR_FOCUSES, ...SUN_FOCUSES],
  paths: { generic: GENERIC_PATH, DEU: DEU_PATH, GBR: GBR_PATH, SUN: SUN_PATH },
  branchPoints: { generic: GENERIC_BRANCHES, DEU: DEU_BRANCHES, GBR: GBR_BRANCHES, SUN: SUN_BRANCHES },
  events: [...GENERIC_EVENTS, ...DEU_EVENTS, ...GBR_EVENTS, ...SUN_EVENTS, ...WORLD_EVENTS],
  decisions: [...GENERIC_DECISIONS, ...NATIONAL_DECISIONS],
  spirits: [...SPIRITS_START, ...SPIRITS_GEN, ...SPIRITS_DEU, ...SPIRITS_GBR, ...SPIRITS_SUN, ...SPIRITS_WORLD],
  traits: TRAITS,
  persons: [...PERSONS_LEADERS, ...PERSONS_DEU, ...PERSONS_GBR, ...PERSONS_SUN],
  beats: HISTORY_BEATS,
  groups: REGION_GROUPS,
  countries: COUNTRY_POLITICS,
  claims: CLAIMS,
  factionNames: FACTION_NAMES,
  templates: [...TEMPLATES_GEN, ...TEMPLATES_DEU, ...TEMPLATES_GBR, ...TEMPLATES_SUN],
  flags: [...FLAGS_BASE, ...FLAGS_GEN, ...FLAGS_DEU, ...FLAGS_GBR, ...FLAGS_SUN, ...FLAGS_WORLD],
  startNaps: START_NAPS,
};
export const CONTENT: ContentRegistry = buildRegistry(CONTENT_PARTS);
```

`content/advisors.ts` — 특성 36개(spec §8.3, 결정 10의 키 이름. 값은 spec 표 그대로; %p 안정도·전쟁지지도는 bp):

- `trainPct`는 P2가 읽지 않던 키다. 기반이 P2 `Training`의 훈련 날수에 `× (100 + stats.trainPct) / 100`(내림, 최소 1)을 더한다(Step 13). spec의 "훈련 시간 −10%"가 이 키다.
- 직함 카드: `title:<trait>`(일반 특성마다 하나, 어느 나라든). 직함 지도자: `title:leader_<ideology>`(이름 키 `ww1936.title.leader_<ideology>`)와 `title:<id>`(K6가 `countries.ts`의 `leaders`에 적는 직함, 예 `title:rou_conducator`). 직함 지도자는 특성이 없다.

`content/treaties.ts`(사실, spec §16.2): `START_NAPS = [["DEU","POL"], ["SUN","POL"], ["SUN","FIN"], ["SUN","EST"], ["SUN","LVA"], ["SUN","LTU"]]`.

`content/history.ts`: `HISTORY_BEATS: HistoryBeat[]` — P0 `HISTORY` 48줄을 **같은 순서로 글자 그대로** 옮긴다(`{ id: "b1939_09_01_deu_pol", date: "1939-09-01", actor: "DEU", act: { kind: "declare", target: "POL" }, safety: true }`; id = `b` + 날짜의 `-`를 `_`로 + `_` + actor·target 소문자). 그 아래 `// ---- new beats (K5, Task 14) ----` 줄만 두고 끝. `HistoryBeats.test.ts` "every P0 calendar entry is a safety beat"가 P0 표와 1:1(날짜·종류·actor·target·순서)을 본다. 표 끝에 새 비트를 더해도 P0 줄의 순서는 그대로다.

- [ ] **Step 8: 정치 상태 `packages/engine/src/ww1936/politics/WwPolitics.ts`** (spec §2.2, §3.3, §22, 결정 1, 2, 9, 13, 17)

```ts
// World War 1936 P3 — politics (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-20-ww1936-p3-politics-design.md).
// One WwPolitics lives in WorldWar next to the economy and the land war.
// WorldWarExecution runs tick() before economy.tick() and tickDeadlines()
// after land.tick(). Everything iterates in smallID / content order.
import { MessageType, PlayerType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import type { PolOrder, WwOrder } from "@openfront/engine-api/Schemas";
import { COUNTRIES, CountryTag, FACTION_IDS, FACTION_LEADER } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { WW_DAY_SPLIT, WwOrderBlock } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/EconomyTypes";
import {
  ADVISOR_SLOTS, DRIFT_KEYS, FactionKey, factionKeyOf, FocusId, FocusTreeId, IDEO_COM, IDEO_DEM,
  IDEO_FAS, IDEO_NEU, IDEOLOGIES, PersonId, RESERVE_KEYS, TraitDef, TraitId, WAR_GOAL_TYPES,
  WarGoalType, WW_BRANCH_DEVIATE_PCT, WW_BRANCH_DEVIATE_POLITICAL_PCT, WW_ELECTION_DAYS,
  WW_EVENT_CHAIN_DEPTH, WW_TENSION_START_BP, WwPoliticsData, WwPolDetail, WwPolGlobal,
  WwPolSummary, WwStats,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/PoliticsTypes";
import { WW_STAT_KEYS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/PoliticsTypes";
import { zInt } from "@openfront/engine-lib/snapshot/SnapshotType";
import { dayOfDate, dayStartTick } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Calendar";
import type { ContentRegistry } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/content/registry";
import {
  dailyPpMilli, effectiveSurrenderPct, LAW_STABILITY_BP, stabilityEffects, warSupportEffects,
} from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/PoliticsRules";
import { idHash, P_BEAT, P_BRANCH, P_ELECTION, wwRoll } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Roll";
import { addStatsInto, clampStats, emptyStats, techStats } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Stats";
import { z } from "zod";
import type { Game, Player } from "../../game/Game";
import type { WorldWar } from "../WorldWar";
import { cancelFocus, focusCards, queueFocus, startFocus, stepFocus, unqueueFocus } from "./Focus";
import { aiPoliticsTick } from "./AiPolitics";
import { fireAdvisor, hireAdvisor } from "./Advisors";
import { cancelMission, decisionCards, stepDecisions, takeDecision } from "./Decisions";
import { chooseEventOption, deliverEvent, expireCards, applyOption, checkTriggeredEvents } from "./Events";
import { dailyFactions, factionOrder } from "./Factions";
import { runBeats } from "./HistoryBeats";
import { dailyPower } from "./Power";
import { dailyTension } from "./Tension";
import { treatyOrder, dailyTreaties } from "./Treaties";
import {
  afterCapitulation, afterWarDeclared, afterWarJoined, cancelJustification, startJustification, stepJustifications,
} from "./WarGoals";

export interface SpiritState { id: string; untilDay: number; addedDay: number }
export interface Mission { id: string; target: number; startDay: number; endDay: number }
export interface Justification { on: number; type: WarGoalType; group: string; progressDays: number; totalDays: number }
export interface WarGoal { on: number; type: WarGoalType; group: string; untilDay: number }
/** `key`: a TechBranch, or a tech id (spec §10.4 research_bonus). */
export interface ResearchBonus { key: string; pct: number; uses: number }
export interface PendingEvent {
  uid: number; eventId: string; from: number; target: number;
  firedTick: number; deadlineTick: number; aiOption: string;
}
export interface FactionState {
  idx: number; name: string; nameTag: CountryTag | ""; leader: number; members: number[]; createdDay: number;
}
export interface Guarantee { by: number; to: number; sinceDay: number }
/** a < b. */
export interface Nap { a: number; b: number; sinceDay: number; startTreaty: boolean }
/** `by`'s armies may enter `from`'s regions (spec §16.3). */
export interface Access { by: number; from: number }
/** a < b. */
export interface OpinionMod { a: number; b: number; value: number; untilDay: number }
/**
 * The delay list (spec §10.5-3, §12.4): "fire" an event, or apply an AI's
 * "choose" late. Kept in (day, seq) order.
 */
export interface Delayed {
  day: number; seq: number; kind: "fire" | "choose"; eventId: string;
  to: number; from: number; target: number; option: string;
}
export interface BeatState { fireDay: number; skipped: boolean; done: boolean }
/** One entry of the tick's event queue (plan decision 13; not state). */
export interface QueuedEvent { eventId: string; to: number; from: number; target: number; depth: number }

/** One country's politics (spec §22.1). Indices: IDEOLOGIES, ADVISOR_SLOTS, RESERVE_KEYS. */
export class CountryPol {
  ppMilli = 50_000;
  ppCumMilli = 0;
  stabBaseBp = 5000;
  wsBaseBp = 3000;
  stabBp = 5000;
  wsBp = 3000;
  pop = [1500, 1000, 2000, 5500];
  ruling = IDEO_NEU;
  leader: PersonId = "";
  tree: FocusTreeId = "generic";
  nextElectionDay = -1;
  electionsSuspended = false;
  crisisDays = [0, 0, 0, 0];
  crisisCooldownUntil = -1;
  spirits: SpiritState[] = [];
  advisors: (string | null)[] = new Array<string | null>(ADVISOR_SLOTS.length).fill(null);
  advisorLockUntil: number[] = new Array<number>(ADVISOR_SLOTS.length).fill(-1);
  /** add_advisor_candidate, ascending. */
  candidates: string[] = [];
  /** remove_advisor: never again (spec §19.6 Eden, Litvinov). Ascending. */
  dismissed: string[] = [];
  /** person_trait, in the order given. */
  personTraits: [string, TraitId][] = [];
  focus: { id: FocusId; progressMilli: number } | null = null;
  queued: FocusId | null = null;
  /** By tree position: 0 open, 1 done, 2 bypassed. */
  focusState: Uint8Array = new Uint8Array(0);
  focusesDone = 0;
  partial = new Map<FocusId, number>();
  /** "<id>" or "<id>@<target smallID>" → first day it can be taken again. */
  decisionCooldown = new Map<string, number>();
  missions: Mission[] = [];
  unlocked = new Set<string>();
  locked = new Set<string>();
  taken = new Set<string>();
  /** flag → [day set, day it lapses or -1]. */
  flags = new Map<string, [number, number]>();
  justification: Justification | null = null;
  warGoals: WarGoal[] = [];
  researchBonuses: ResearchBonus[] = [];
  extraResearchSlots = 0;
  reserved: number[] = new Array<number>(RESERVE_KEYS.length).fill(0);
  pending: PendingEvent[] = [];
  firedOnce = new Set<string>();
  /** Repeating event → first day it may fire again. */
  eventCooldown = new Map<string, number>();
  /** improve_relations: target → first day again. */
  improveCooldown = new Map<number, number>();
  /** Countries this one declared war on and still fights (spec §6.1 offensive line; D keeps it). Ascending. */
  declaredOn: number[] = [];
  /** Spec §3.3, state; CountryEconomy.stats is this same object (plan decision 9). */
  readonly stats: WwStats = emptyStats();
  /** Derived (DERIVED_FIELDS): tech, laws, spirits, advisors, government, leader, dynamic. */
  statParts: WwStats[] = [];

  constructor(readonly smallID: number, readonly tag: CountryTag) {}

  hasFlag(f: string, day: number): boolean {
    const v = this.flags.get(f);
    return v !== undefined && (v[1] < 0 || day < v[1]);
  }
  hasSpirit(id: string): boolean {
    return this.spirits.some((s) => s.id === id);
  }
}

/** Spec §5.3 government stats. */
const GOV_STATS: readonly Partial<WwStats>[] = [
  { stabilityBp: 500, justifyCostPct: 50 },
  { ppGainPct: 5 },
  { warSupportBp: 500, justifyTimePct: -10 },
  {},
];

export class WwPolitics {
  countries: (CountryPol | null)[];
  // ---- world state (spec §22.1 "global") ----
  wtBp = WW_TENSION_START_BP;
  threatBp: number[];
  factions: FactionState[] = [];
  /** smallID → faction idx, -1: none. Derived from `factions` (rebuildDerived). */
  factionOfIdx: number[];
  nextFactionIdx = FACTION_IDS.length;
  guarantees: Guarantee[] = [];
  naps: Nap[] = [];
  access: Access[] = [];
  opinions: OpinionMod[] = [];
  readonly legalOwner: Uint16Array;
  /** 0: the scenario's core flag, 1: core of its legal owner, 2: not core (spec §17.6). */
  readonly coreOverride: Uint8Array;
  gflags = new Map<string, number>();
  firedOnceGlobal = new Set<string>();
  removedGenerals = new Set<string>();
  delayed: Delayed[] = [];
  nextSeq = 1;
  beats: BeatState[] = [];
  /** "<tree or tag>:<branch point id>" → the alternative taken, null: history. Insertion = roll order. */
  branchDeviations = new Map<string, FocusId | null>();
  nextUid = 1;
  private _seed = 0;
  private _rolled = false;
  private lastCommonDay = -1;
  // ---- derived (DERIVED_FIELDS) ----
  private eventQueue: QueuedEvent[] = [];
  private newsOut: WwPoliticsData["news"] = [];
  private cardsOut: WwPoliticsData["cards"] = [];
  private dirtyPolCountries = new Set<number>();
  private dirtyPolRegions = new Set<number>();
  private polGlobalDirty = true;

  constructor(readonly mg: Game, readonly ww: WorldWar, readonly content: ContentRegistry) {
    const sc = ww.scenario;
    const n = sc.countries.length + 1;
    this.countries = [null, ...sc.countries.map((tag, i) => new CountryPol(i + 1, tag))];
    this.threatBp = new Array<number>(n).fill(0);
    this.factionOfIdx = new Array<number>(n).fill(-1);
    const size = sc.maxRegionId() + 1;
    this.legalOwner = new Uint16Array(size);
    this.coreOverride = new Uint8Array(size);
    const owners = sc.ownerByRegion();
    for (const r of sc.landRegions) this.legalOwner[r.id] = owners[r.id];
    // Spec §15.1: the starting factions, FACTION_IDS order.
    FACTION_IDS.forEach((f, idx) => {
      const members = sc.countries.flatMap((t, i) => (COUNTRIES[t].faction === f ? [i + 1] : []));
      if (members.length === 0) return;
      const lead = sc.smallIdOf(FACTION_LEADER[f]);
      this.factions.push({ idx, name: f, nameTag: "", leader: members.includes(lead) ? lead : members[0], members, createdDay: 0 });
    });
    for (const c of this.list()) c.tree = this.content.countryIdx.get(c.tag)?.tree ?? "generic";
    this.rebuildDerived();
  }

  get econ() { return this.ww.economy; }
  get land() { return this.ww.land; }
  get seed(): number { return this._seed; }
  rolled(): boolean { return this._rolled; }

  /** Spec §6.5, §9.2, §19.10: starting values. WorldWar.start calls it once, after land.start. */
  start(): void {
    for (const c of this.list()) {
      const d = this.content.countryIdx.get(c.tag);
      const ruling = d?.ruling ?? "neutral";
      c.ruling = IDEOLOGIES.indexOf(ruling);
      if (d !== undefined) {
        c.pop = d.popularity.map((p) => p * 100);
        c.stabBaseBp = d.stability * 100;
        c.wsBaseBp = d.warSupport * 100;
        if (d.firstElection !== undefined) c.nextElectionDay = dayOfDate(d.firstElection);
        for (const s of d.spirits ?? []) c.spirits.push({ id: s, untilDay: -1, addedDay: 0 });
      } else if (c.ruling === IDEO_NEU) {
        c.pop = [1500, 1000, 2000, 5500];
      }
      c.leader = d?.leaders[ruling] ?? `title:leader_${ruling}`;
      c.stabBp = c.stabBaseBp;
      c.wsBp = c.wsBaseBp;
      c.focusState = new Uint8Array(this.content.treeFocuses[c.tree].length);
      this.recomputeStats(c.smallID);
    }
    for (const [x, y] of this.content.startNaps) {
      const a = this.idOfTag(x);
      const b = this.idOfTag(y);
      if (a !== 0 && b !== 0) this.naps.push({ a: Math.min(a, b), b: Math.max(a, b), sinceDay: 0, startTreaty: true });
    }
    this.polGlobalDirty = true;
  }

  /**
   * Plan decision 2: the seed, the history beats (P0 decision 14's 1-in-10
   * skip and 0–20 days, plan decision 16), the branch deviations (spec
   * §18.4) and the hashed first elections (spec §5.4). Once a game.
   */
  roll(seed: number, historical: boolean): void {
    if (this._rolled) return;
    this._rolled = true;
    this._seed = seed | 0;
    this.beats = this.content.beats.map((b, i) => {
      const h = this.content.beatHash[i];
      const skipped = !historical || wwRoll(seed, P_BEAT, h, 1) % 10 === 0;
      return { fireDay: dayOfDate(b.date) + (wwRoll(seed, P_BEAT, h, 2) % 21), skipped, done: false };
    });
    for (const c of this.list()) {
      const tree = c.tree;
      for (const bp of this.content.branchPoints[tree] ?? []) {
        const pct = bp.political ? WW_BRANCH_DEVIATE_POLITICAL_PCT : WW_BRANCH_DEVIATE_PCT;
        const a = idHash(c.tag);
        const b = idHash(bp.id);
        let alt: FocusId | null = null;
        if (historical && bp.alternatives.length > 0 && wwRoll(seed, P_BRANCH, a, b) % 100 < pct) {
          alt = bp.alternatives[wwRoll(seed, P_BRANCH, a, b, 1) % bp.alternatives.length];
        }
        this.branchDeviations.set(`${c.tag}:${bp.id}`, alt);
      }
      if (c.ruling === IDEO_DEM && c.nextElectionDay < 0) {
        c.nextElectionDay = wwRoll(seed, P_ELECTION, c.smallID) % WW_ELECTION_DAYS;
      }
    }
  }

  // ---- Clock (spec §2.2) ----

  /** Order 2 and 3: the day's common step, then this tick's share of countries. */
  tick(ticks: number): void {
    if (this.ww.inPrep(ticks)) return;
    const day = this.ww.day(ticks);
    if (day !== this.lastCommonDay) {
      this.lastCommonDay = day;
      this.dailyCommon(day, ticks);
    }
    const k = ticks - dayStartTick(day, this.ww.prepEndTick());
    if (k < 0 || k >= WW_DAY_SPLIT) return;
    for (let id = 1; id < this.countries.length; id++) {
      if (id % WW_DAY_SPLIT === k) this.dailyCountry(id, day, ticks);
    }
  }

  private dailyCommon(day: number, tick: number): void {
    dailyTension(this, day);
    // The delay list: what is due, in (day, seq) order (spec §12.3).
    while (this.delayed.length > 0 && this.delayed[0].day <= day) {
      const d = this.delayed.shift()!;
      if (d.kind === "fire") this.queueEvent(d.eventId, d.to, d.from, d.target, 0);
      else applyOption(this, d.eventId, d.to, d.from, d.target, d.option, tick, 0);
      this.flushEvents(tick);
    }
    for (const e of this.content.dateEvents.get(day) ?? []) {
      const def = this.content.events[e];
      for (const tag of [...(def.date?.to ?? [])].sort()) {
        const to = this.idOfTag(tag);
        if (to !== 0) this.queueEvent(def.id, to, to, 0, 0);
      }
    }
    this.flushEvents(tick);
    runBeats(this, day, tick);
    this.flushEvents(tick);
    dailyFactions(this, day, tick);
    dailyTreaties(this, day, tick);
    stepJustifications(this, day, tick);
    this.flushEvents(tick);
  }

  private dailyCountry(id: number, day: number, tick: number): void {
    const c = this.countries[id];
    if (c === null || c === undefined) return;
    if (!this.living(id)) {
      if (c.pending.length > 0) c.pending = []; // spec §12.5
      return;
    }
    dailyPower(this, id, day, tick);
    stepFocus(this, id, day, tick);
    stepDecisions(this, id, day, tick);
    if ((day + id) % 5 === 0) checkTriggeredEvents(this, id, day, tick);
    if (!this.isHuman(id)) aiPoliticsTick(this, id, day, tick);
    this.flushEvents(tick);
    this.markCountry(id);
  }

  /** Order 6: human cards past their deadline get their shown option (spec §12.5). */
  tickDeadlines(tick: number): void {
    expireCards(this, tick);
    this.flushEvents(tick);
  }

  // ---- Orders (spec §22.3; the same for humans and the AI) ----

  issue(smallID: number, order: WwOrder, tick: number): WwOrderBlock | null {
    if (!this.living(smallID)) return "dead";
    const o = order as PolOrder;
    let block: WwOrderBlock | null;
    switch (o.kind) {
      case "focus_start": block = startFocus(this, smallID, o.focus, tick); break;
      case "focus_queue": block = queueFocus(this, smallID, o.focus); break;
      case "focus_cancel": block = cancelFocus(this, smallID); break;
      case "focus_unqueue": block = unqueueFocus(this, smallID); break;
      case "advisor_hire": block = hireAdvisor(this, smallID, o.slot, o.advisor, this.day()); break;
      case "advisor_fire": block = fireAdvisor(this, smallID, o.slot, this.day()); break;
      case "decision_take": block = takeDecision(this, smallID, o.decision, o.target, tick, false); break;
      case "decision_cancel": block = cancelMission(this, smallID, o.decision, o.target); break;
      case "event_choose": block = chooseEventOption(this, smallID, o.uid, o.option, tick); break;
      case "justify": block = startJustification(this, smallID, o.target, o.goal, o.group, tick); break;
      case "justify_cancel": block = cancelJustification(this, smallID); break;
      case "guarantee": case "guarantee_end": case "nap_offer": case "nap_break":
      case "access_ask": case "access_revoke": case "improve_relations":
        block = treatyOrder(this, smallID, o, tick); break;
      case "faction_create": case "faction_invite": case "faction_request": case "faction_kick":
      case "faction_leave": case "faction_dissolve":
        block = factionOrder(this, smallID, o, tick); break;
      default: return "bad_index";
    }
    this.flushEvents(tick);
    if (block === null) this.markCountry(smallID);
    return block;
  }

  // ---- Stats (spec §3.3) ----

  stats(smallID: number): WwStats {
    return this.countries[smallID]?.stats ?? emptyStats();
  }

  /** Every source again for one country. Keeps the stats object (the economy holds it). */
  recomputeStats(smallID: number): void {
    const c = this.countries[smallID];
    const ce = this.econ.country(smallID);
    if (c === null || c === undefined || ce === null) return;
    const tech = techStats(ce.tech);
    const laws = emptyStats();
    for (const v of [ce.laws.economy, ce.laws.trade, ce.laws.conscription]) laws.stabilityBp += LAW_STABILITY_BP[v] ?? 0;
    const spirits = emptyStats();
    for (const s of c.spirits) {
      const d = this.content.spiritIdx.get(s.id);
      if (d !== undefined) addStatsInto(spirits, d.stats);
    }
    const atWar = this.ww.hasWar(smallID);
    const advisors = emptyStats();
    for (const a of c.advisors) if (a !== null) for (const t of this.traitsOf(c, a)) this.addTrait(advisors, t, c, atWar);
    const gov = emptyStats();
    addStatsInto(gov, GOV_STATS[c.ruling]);
    const leader = emptyStats();
    for (const t of this.leaderTraits(c)) this.addTrait(leader, t, c, atWar);
    const dyn = emptyStats();
    addStatsInto(dyn, stabilityEffects(c.stabBp));
    addStatsInto(dyn, warSupportEffects(c.wsBp));
    const parts = [tech, laws, spirits, advisors, gov, leader, dyn];
    const total = emptyStats();
    for (const p of parts) addStatsInto(total, p);
    clampStats(total);
    for (const k of WW_STAT_KEYS) c.stats[k] = total[k];
    c.statParts = parts;
    this.markCountry(smallID);
  }

  private addTrait(out: WwStats, t: TraitDef, c: CountryPol, atWar: boolean): void {
    if (t.atWarOnly === true && !atWar) return;
    if (t.ideology !== undefined && IDEOLOGIES[c.ruling] !== t.ideology) return;
    addStatsInto(out, t.stats);
    if (t.rulingDriftBp !== undefined) out[DRIFT_KEYS[c.ruling]] += t.rulingDriftBp;
  }

  /** An advisor's traits: a person's (with person_trait), or a titled card's one. */
  traitsOf(c: CountryPol, advisor: string): TraitDef[] {
    const ids: TraitId[] = [];
    if (advisor.startsWith("title:")) ids.push(advisor.slice(6) as TraitId);
    else {
      ids.push(...(this.content.personIdx.get(advisor)?.advisor?.traits ?? []));
      for (const [p, t] of c.personTraits) if (p === advisor && !ids.includes(t)) ids.push(t);
    }
    return ids.flatMap((id) => this.content.traitIdx.get(id) ?? []);
  }

  leaderTraits(c: CountryPol): TraitDef[] {
    const p = this.content.personIdx.get(c.leader);
    return (p?.leaderTraits ?? []).flatMap((id) => this.content.traitIdx.get(id) ?? []);
  }

  surrenderPct(smallID: number): number {
    const c = this.countries[smallID];
    const base = COUNTRIES[this.tagOf(smallID)].surrenderPct;
    return c ? effectiveSurrenderPct(base, c.stabBp, c.wsBp, c.stats.surrenderPp) : base;
  }

  dailyGainMilli(smallID: number): number {
    return dailyPpMilli(this.stats(smallID));
  }

  // ---- Event queue (plan decision 13, spec §10.5) ----

  queueEvent(eventId: string, to: number, from: number, target: number, depth: number): void {
    this.eventQueue.push({ eventId, to, from, target, depth });
  }

  /** Empties the tick's queue; deeper than WW_EVENT_CHAIN_DEPTH waits for tomorrow. */
  flushEvents(tick: number): void {
    while (this.eventQueue.length > 0) {
      const q = this.eventQueue.shift()!;
      if (q.depth > WW_EVENT_CHAIN_DEPTH) {
        this.addDelayed({ day: this.day() + 1, kind: "fire", eventId: q.eventId, to: q.to, from: q.from, target: q.target, option: "" });
        continue;
      }
      deliverEvent(this, q, tick);
    }
  }

  queuedEvents(): number {
    return this.eventQueue.length;
  }

  addDelayed(d: Omit<Delayed, "seq">): void {
    const e: Delayed = { ...d, seq: this.nextSeq++ };
    let i = this.delayed.length;
    while (i > 0 && this.delayed[i - 1].day > e.day) i--;
    this.delayed.splice(i, 0, e);
  }

  // ---- Land (spec §17, plan decision 17) ----

  /** The only way a legal owner changes. `core`: undefined keeps the override. */
  setLegalOwner(region: number, to: number, core?: boolean): void {
    if (this.legalOwner[region] === to && core === undefined) return;
    this.legalOwner[region] = to;
    if (core !== undefined) this.coreOverride[region] = core ? 1 : 2;
    this.dirtyPolRegions.add(region);
    this.econ.markRegionDirty(region);
  }

  isCore(region: number): boolean {
    const o = this.coreOverride[region];
    return o === 0 ? (this.ww.scenario.region(region)?.core ?? true) : o === 1;
  }

  // ---- Queries ----

  list(): CountryPol[] {
    return this.countries.filter((c): c is CountryPol => c !== null);
  }
  country(smallID: number): CountryPol | null {
    return this.countries[smallID] ?? null;
  }
  tagOf(smallID: number): CountryTag {
    return this.ww.tagOf(smallID);
  }
  /** 0 when the tag is not in this scenario. */
  idOfTag(tag: CountryTag): number {
    return this.ww.scenario.smallIdOf(tag);
  }
  player(smallID: number): Player | null {
    const p = this.mg.playerBySmallID(smallID);
    return p.isPlayer() ? p : null;
  }
  living(smallID: number): boolean {
    const p = this.player(smallID);
    return p !== null && p.isAlive() && !this.ww.isCapitulated(smallID);
  }
  isHuman(smallID: number): boolean {
    return this.player(smallID)?.type() === PlayerType.Human;
  }
  day(): number {
    return this.ww.day(this.mg.ticks());
  }
  factionIdx(smallID: number): number {
    return this.factionOfIdx[smallID] ?? -1;
  }
  faction(idx: number): FactionState | null {
    return this.factions.find((f) => f.idx === idx) ?? null;
  }
  factionOf(smallID: number): FactionKey | null {
    const i = this.factionIdx(smallID);
    return i < 0 ? null : factionKeyOf(i);
  }
  sameFaction(a: number, b: number): boolean {
    return a !== b && this.factionIdx(a) >= 0 && this.factionIdx(a) === this.factionIdx(b);
  }
  /** Ideology of a faction = its leader's ruling ideology (spec §5.3). */
  factionIdeology(idx: number): number {
    const f = this.faction(idx);
    return f === null ? IDEO_NEU : (this.countries[f.leader]?.ruling ?? IDEO_NEU);
  }
  hasAccess(by: number, from: number): boolean {
    return this.access.some((x) => x.by === by && x.from === from);
  }
  hasWarGoal(by: number, on: number): boolean {
    return this.countries[by]?.warGoals.some((g) => g.on === on) ?? false;
  }
  isAtWarBlockedByNap(a: number, b: number): boolean {
    const lo = Math.min(a, b);
    const hi = Math.max(a, b);
    return this.naps.some((n) => n.a === lo && n.b === hi);
  }
  guarantorsOf(to: number): number[] {
    return this.guarantees.filter((g) => g.to === to).map((g) => g.by);
  }
  generalRemoved(id: string): boolean {
    return this.removedGenerals.has(id);
  }
  /** Plan decision 19: a person serving as an advisor somewhere is not a general meanwhile. */
  advisorHolds(generalId: string): boolean {
    for (const c of this.list()) {
      for (const a of c.advisors) {
        if (a !== null && this.content.personIdx.get(a)?.alsoGeneral === generalId) return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }
  /**
   * Spec §18.7: whom `smallID` plans to fight within 60 days (P2 AI): due
   * history beats, a justification about to finish, held war goals. Ascending.
   */
  plannedWars(smallID: number, day: number): number[] {
    const out = new Set<number>();
    this.content.beats.forEach((b, i) => {
      const s = this.beats[i];
      if (s === undefined || s.skipped || s.done || s.fireDay < day || s.fireDay > day + 60) return;
      if (b.act.kind !== "declare" && b.act.kind !== "join") return;
      const a = this.idOfTag(b.actor);
      const t = this.idOfTag(b.act.target);
      if (a === smallID && t !== 0) out.add(t);
      if (t === smallID && a !== 0) out.add(a);
    });
    const c = this.countries[smallID];
    const j = c?.justification ?? null;
    if (j !== null && j.totalDays - j.progressDays <= 60) out.add(j.on);
    for (const g of c?.warGoals ?? []) out.add(g.on);
    return [...out].sort((x, y) => x - y);
  }

  // ---- Hooks from P0 (Diplomacy, Capitulation) ----

  onWarDeclared(attacker: number, target: number, joined: readonly number[], noGoal: boolean, tick: number): void {
    afterWarDeclared(this, attacker, target, joined, noGoal, tick);
    for (const id of [attacker, ...joined]) this.recomputeStats(id); // war_leader, at-war lines
    this.flushEvents(tick);
    this.polGlobalDirty = true;
  }

  onWarJoined(actor: number, enemy: number, tick: number): void {
    afterWarJoined(this, actor, enemy, tick);
    this.recomputeStats(actor);
    this.flushEvents(tick);
    this.polGlobalDirty = true;
  }

  /** Spec §17.1: the loser's legal regions go to the victor; its cards vanish (spec §12.5). */
  onCapitulated(loser: number, victor: number, tick: number): void {
    if (victor !== 0) {
      for (const r of this.ww.scenario.landRegions) {
        if (this.legalOwner[r.id] === loser) this.setLegalOwner(r.id, victor, false);
      }
    }
    const c = this.countries[loser];
    if (c !== null && c !== undefined) {
      c.pending = [];
      c.focus = null;
      c.queued = null;
      c.justification = null;
    }
    afterCapitulation(this, loser, victor, tick);
    this.flushEvents(tick);
    this.polGlobalDirty = true;
  }

  // ---- Messages, news, change marks ----

  /** A message to the country's human player only. */
  message(smallID: number, key: string, params: Record<string, string | number> = {}): void {
    const p = this.player(smallID);
    if (p === null || p.type() !== PlayerType.Human) return;
    this.mg.displayMessage(`events_display.${key}`, MessageType.WORLD_WAR, p.id(), undefined, params);
  }
  messageAll(key: string, params: Record<string, string | number> = {}): void {
    this.mg.displayMessage(`events_display.${key}`, MessageType.WORLD_WAR, null, undefined, params);
  }
  /** Spec §12.7: world news, this tick's update only. */
  news(id: string, params: Record<string, string | number> = {}): void {
    this.newsOut.push({ id, params });
  }
  /** E: a new human card for the receiver's screen. */
  noteCard(to: number, card: WwPoliticsData["cards"][number]): void {
    this.cardsOut.push({ ...card, to });
  }
  markCountry(smallID: number): void {
    this.dirtyPolCountries.add(smallID);
  }
  markGlobal(): void {
    this.polGlobalDirty = true;
  }

  // ---- Screens (spec §22.4) ----

  takeData(): WwPoliticsData | null {
    if (this.dirtyPolCountries.size === 0 && !this.polGlobalDirty && this.dirtyPolRegions.size === 0
      && this.newsOut.length === 0 && this.cardsOut.length === 0) return null;
    const ids = [...this.dirtyPolCountries].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const d: WwPoliticsData = {
      countries: ids.flatMap((id) => (this.countries[id] ? [this.summary(this.countries[id]!)] : [])),
      details: ids.flatMap((id) => (this.countries[id] && this.isHuman(id) ? [this.detail(this.countries[id]!)] : [])),
      global: this.polGlobalDirty ? this.globalData() : null,
      regions: [...this.dirtyPolRegions].sort((a, b) => a - b).map((id) => ({ id, legalOwner: this.legalOwner[id] })),
      news: this.newsOut,
      cards: this.cardsOut,
    };
    this.dirtyPolCountries.clear();
    this.dirtyPolRegions.clear();
    this.polGlobalDirty = false;
    this.newsOut = [];
    this.cardsOut = [];
    return d;
  }

  summary(c: CountryPol): WwPolSummary { /* below */ }
  detail(c: CountryPol): WwPolDetail { /* below */ }
  globalData(): WwPolGlobal { /* below */ }

  // ---- Snapshot, hash (spec §22.5) ----

  snapshot(): WwPoliticsState { /* below */ }
  restoreState(s: WwPoliticsState): void { /* below */ }

  /** Factions' reverse index, the economy's stats alias and tech hook, P2 sides. */
  rebuildDerived(): void {
    this.factionOfIdx.fill(-1);
    for (const f of this.factions) for (const m of f.members) this.factionOfIdx[m] = f.idx;
    for (const c of this.list()) {
      const ce = this.econ.country(c.smallID);
      if (ce === null) continue;
      ce.stats = c.stats;
      ce.onTechChanged = (id) => this.recomputeStats(id);
    }
    this.eventQueue = [];
  }

  /** Spec §15.3: call after any faction change (sides, trade partners). */
  factionsChanged(): void {
    this.rebuildDerived();
    this.ww.land?.onFactionsChanged();
    this.polGlobalDirty = true;
  }

  hash(): number { /* below */ }
}
```

`/* below */` 본문은 다음을 그대로 계산한다:
- `summary(c)`: `{smallID, ruling, leader, pop: [...c.pop], stabBp, wsBp, ppMilli: 사람 나라면 c.ppMilli 아니면 0, faction: factionIdx, surrenderPct: this.surrenderPct(id), focus: c.focus?.id ?? null}`.
- `detail(c)`: `ppGainMilli = dailyGainMilli`, 중점(`daysMilli = def.days × 1000`), `cards = focusCards(this, id)`(F), 고문·잠금·후보(사람이 고를 수 있는 사람 id: `candidatesFor(this, id, day)`는 P의 `Advisors.ts` 함수 — 기반 스텁은 `[]`), 정신, `decisions = decisionCards(this, id)`(E), 임무, 정당화(`blocked`: `justifyGate`가 지금 막는지 — D의 `justificationBlocked(this, id)`, 스텁 `false`), 목표, 연구 보너스, `statParts`(`WW_STAT_KEYS` 순서 숫자 배열 7개), `stats`(숫자 배열), 바탕 값, 선거일, 위기 날수, `pending`(카드 모양, `open`은 E의 `openOptions(this, id, p)` — 스텁 모든 선택지 id).
- `globalData()`: `wtBp`, `threat` = `threatBp` 큰 순 10개(같으면 작은 smallID, 0은 뺌), 진영(`name`, `nameTag`, `key: factionKeyOf(idx)`), 보장 `[by, to]`, 불가침 `[a, b, sinceDay]`, 통행 `[by, from]`, `justifying` = 모든 나라의 정당화 `[by, on, totalDays − progressDays]`(smallID 순), `nextBeatDay` = 빠지지 않고 끝나지 않은 비트 중 가장 이른 `fireDay`(없으면 −1).
- `hash()`: 나라마다(smallID 순) `ppMilli`, `stabBp`, `wsBp`, `pop[0..3]`, `ruling`, `focusesDone`, `pending.length`, `advisors`의 null 아닌 수; 그다음 `wtBp`, 진영마다 `idx × 97 + members.length`, `guarantees.length`, `naps.length`, `access.length`, `legalOwner`가 `scenario.ownerByRegion()`과 다른 주 수, `delayed.length`, `beats`의 `done` 수. `Math.imul(h, 31) + v | 0` 누적, `>>> 0`.
- `snapshot()`/`restoreState()`: 아래 스키마의 모든 필드. `Set`·`Map`은 **정렬한 배열**로(문자열은 사전 순, 숫자는 오름차순). `focusState`는 `done: string[]`, `bypassed: string[]`(그 트리의 id, 트리 순서)로 쓰고 복원 때 `treePos`로 되돌린다(없는 id는 버린다, spec §22.5). `stats`는 `WW_STAT_KEYS` 순서 숫자 배열이고 복원은 `c.stats[k] = …`(별칭 유지). `restoreState`는 대입 → `rebuildDerived()` → 나라마다 `recomputeStats`(같은 값이 다시 써진다; `statParts`만 다시 생긴다) → 모든 나라·세계·주를 "바뀜"으로. `politics`가 없는 스냅샷은 `WorldWar.restoreState`가 거절한다(결정 30).

```ts
const zStr = () => z.string();
const CountryPolSchema = z.object({
  smallID: zInt(), ppMilli: zInt(), ppCumMilli: zInt(),
  stabBaseBp: zInt(), wsBaseBp: zInt(), stabBp: zInt(), wsBp: zInt(),
  pop: z.array(zInt()), ruling: zInt(), leader: zStr(), tree: z.enum(["generic", "DEU", "GBR", "SUN"]),
  nextElectionDay: zInt(), electionsSuspended: z.boolean(), crisisDays: z.array(zInt()), crisisCooldownUntil: zInt(),
  spirits: z.array(z.object({ id: zStr(), untilDay: zInt(), addedDay: zInt() })),
  advisors: z.array(zStr().nullable()), advisorLockUntil: z.array(zInt()),
  candidates: z.array(zStr()), dismissed: z.array(zStr()), personTraits: z.array(z.tuple([zStr(), zStr()])),
  focus: z.object({ id: zStr(), progressMilli: zInt() }).nullable(), queued: zStr().nullable(),
  done: z.array(zStr()), bypassed: z.array(zStr()), focusesDone: zInt(),
  partial: z.array(z.tuple([zStr(), zInt()])),
  decisionCooldown: z.array(z.tuple([zStr(), zInt()])),
  missions: z.array(z.object({ id: zStr(), target: zInt(), startDay: zInt(), endDay: zInt() })),
  unlocked: z.array(zStr()), locked: z.array(zStr()), taken: z.array(zStr()),
  flags: z.array(z.tuple([zStr(), zInt(), zInt()])),
  justification: z.object({ on: zInt(), type: z.enum(WAR_GOAL_TYPES), group: zStr(), progressDays: zInt(), totalDays: zInt() }).nullable(),
  warGoals: z.array(z.object({ on: zInt(), type: z.enum(WAR_GOAL_TYPES), group: zStr(), untilDay: zInt() })),
  researchBonuses: z.array(z.object({ key: zStr(), pct: zInt(), uses: zInt() })),
  extraResearchSlots: zInt(), reserved: z.array(zInt()),
  pending: z.array(z.object({ uid: zInt(), eventId: zStr(), from: zInt(), target: zInt(), firedTick: zInt(), deadlineTick: zInt(), aiOption: zStr() })),
  firedOnce: z.array(zStr()), eventCooldown: z.array(z.tuple([zStr(), zInt()])),
  improveCooldown: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), zInt()])),
  declaredOn: z.array(zInt()),
  stats: z.array(zInt()),
});
export const WwPoliticsStateSchema = z.object({
  seed: zInt(), rolled: z.boolean(), lastCommonDay: zInt(),
  countries: z.array(CountryPolSchema),
  wtBp: zInt(), threatBp: z.array(zInt()),
  factions: z.array(z.object({ idx: zInt(), name: zStr(), nameTag: zStr(), leader: zInt(), members: z.array(zInt()), createdDay: zInt() })),
  nextFactionIdx: zInt(),
  guarantees: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), zInt(), zInt()])),
  naps: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), zInt(), zInt(), z.boolean()])),
  access: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), zInt()])),
  opinions: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), zInt(), zInt(), zInt()])),
  legalOwner: z.array(zInt()), coreOverride: z.array(zInt()),
  gflags: z.array(z.tuple([zStr(), zInt()])), firedOnceGlobal: z.array(zStr()), removedGenerals: z.array(zStr()),
  delayed: z.array(z.object({ day: zInt(), seq: zInt(), kind: z.enum(["fire", "choose"]), eventId: zStr(), to: zInt(), from: zInt(), target: zInt(), option: zStr() })),
  nextSeq: zInt(),
  beats: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), z.boolean(), z.boolean()])),
  branchDeviations: z.array(z.tuple([zStr(), zStr().nullable()])),
  nextUid: zInt(),
});
export type WwPoliticsState = z.infer<typeof WwPoliticsStateSchema>;
```

- 비트는 콘텐츠 순서의 배열이라 콘텐츠를 더하면 줄이 어긋난다. 그래서 `beats`는 **비트 id와 함께** 저장하지 않는 대신, 복원은 저장된 줄 수까지만 순서대로 대입하고 새 비트(콘텐츠가 더 긴 경우)는 `skipped: true`로 둔다 *(결정: 판 도중의 콘텐츠 갱신은 없다 — 서버와 클라이언트가 같은 빌드다. 길이가 다르면 경고 없이 위 규칙)*.

- [ ] **Step 9: 효과 분배와 조건 평가 (`Dispatch.ts`, `Conditions.ts`, `ContentSource.ts`)** (spec §10.2, §10.3, §10.5, 결정 7, 12)

`who`가 바꾸는 것은 **효과를 받는 나라(actor)**뿐이다: 효과 안의 다른 선택자(`target`, `of`, `to`)는 원래 문맥(`ctx.self`, `ctx.from`, `ctx.target`)으로 푼다. 그래서 spec §20.3의 `{ kind: "annex", who: "from", target: "self" }`(독일이 오스트리아를 합병)가 뜻대로 된다. 무리 함수는 spec §26.3의 `(ctx, e)`가 아니라 **`(ctx, e, actor)`**를 받는다.

`packages/engine/src/ww1936/politics/Dispatch.ts`:

```ts
import { CountryTag } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { Effect, effectGroup, Selector } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/PoliticsDsl";
import { applyDiploEffect } from "./EffectsDiplo";
import { applyEconEffect } from "./EffectsEcon";
import { applyEventEffect } from "./EffectsEvent";
import { applyPoliticalEffect } from "./EffectsPol";
import { applyTerritoryEffect } from "./Territory";
import type { WwPolitics } from "./WwPolitics";

/**
 * Spec §10.2: who the data talks about. self: the focus / decision owner or
 * the event receiver; from: the event's sender; target: the decision's or
 * request's target. depth: the event chain length (spec §10.5-3).
 */
export interface EffectCtx {
  pol: WwPolitics;
  self: number;
  from: number;
  target: number;
  day: number;
  tick: number;
  depth: number;
}

export function makeCtx(pol: WwPolitics, self: number, from: number, target: number, tick: number, depth = 0): EffectCtx {
  return { pol, self, from, target, day: pol.ww.day(tick), tick, depth };
}

/** The smallID a selector names; 0 when nobody (not in this scenario, no faction). Dead countries resolve. */
export function resolveSelector(ctx: EffectCtx, s: Selector | undefined): number {
  if (s === undefined || s === "self") return ctx.self;
  if (s === "from") return ctx.from;
  if (s === "target") return ctx.target;
  if ("tag" in s) return ctx.pol.idOfTag(s.tag as CountryTag);
  if ("factionLeaderOf" in s) {
    const f = ctx.pol.faction(ctx.pol.factionIdx(resolveSelector(ctx, s.factionLeaderOf)));
    return f?.leader ?? 0;
  }
  const g = ctx.pol.content.groupIdx.get(s.ownerOf);
  return g === undefined || g.regions.length === 0 ? 0 : ctx.pol.legalOwner[g.regions[0]] ?? 0;
}

/** Spec §10.5-1/-2: in order; an effect whose actor is missing or dead is skipped. */
export function applyEffects(ctx: EffectCtx, effects: readonly Effect[]): void {
  for (const e of effects) applyEffect(ctx, e);
}

export function applyEffect(ctx: EffectCtx, e: Effect): void {
  const actor = e.who === undefined ? ctx.self : resolveSelector(ctx, e.who);
  if (actor === 0 || !ctx.pol.living(actor)) return;
  switch (effectGroup(e.kind)) {
    case "event": applyEventEffect(ctx, e, actor); break;
    case "political": applyPoliticalEffect(ctx, e, actor); break;
    case "econ": applyEconEffect(ctx, e, actor); break;
    case "diplo": applyDiploEffect(ctx, e, actor); break;
    case "territory": applyTerritoryEffect(ctx, e, actor); break;
  }
}
```

`packages/engine/src/ww1936/politics/Conditions.ts`:

```ts
import { COUNTRIES } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { LAW_LADDERS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/EconomyTypes";
import type { Condition, LeafCondition } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/PoliticsDsl";
import { IDEOLOGIES } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/PoliticsTypes";
import { dayOfDate } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Calendar";
import { conditionLeaves } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Describe";
import { EffectCtx, resolveSelector } from "./Dispatch";
import { opinionOf } from "./Treaties";
import type { WwPolitics } from "./WwPolitics";

export function evalCondition(ctx: EffectCtx, c: Condition | undefined): boolean {
  if (c === undefined) return true;
  if ("all" in c) return c.all.every((x) => evalCondition(ctx, x));
  if ("any" in c) return c.any.some((x) => evalCondition(ctx, x));
  if ("not" in c) return !evalCondition(ctx, c.not);
  return evalLeaf(ctx, c);
}

/** Leaf numbers (conditionLeaves order) that are false now: the screens' ✗ lines (spec §21.3). */
export function failingLeaves(ctx: EffectCtx, c: Condition | undefined): number[] {
  const out: number[] = [];
  conditionLeaves(c).forEach((l, i) => {
    if (!evalLeaf(ctx, l)) out.push(i);
  });
  return out;
}

export function divisionCount(pol: WwPolitics, id: number): number {
  const t = pol.land.divisions;
  let n = 0;
  for (let r = 0; r < t.rows; r++) if (t.id[r] !== 0 && t.owner[r] === id) n++;
  return n;
}

/** Spec §18.7: Σ division strength ‰ (P2 strengthOf). */
export function armyStrength(pol: WwPolitics, id: number): number {
  const t = pol.land.divisions;
  let n = 0;
  for (let r = 0; r < t.rows; r++) if (t.id[r] !== 0 && t.owner[r] === id) n += pol.land.strengthOf(r);
  return n;
}

export function capitalRegion(pol: WwPolitics, id: number): number {
  const sc = pol.ww.scenario;
  return sc.regionAt(sc.capitalTile(pol.tagOf(id)));
}

/** Spec §6.1, §6.4: % of the legal victory points someone else controls. */
export function lostLegalVpPct(pol: WwPolitics, id: number): number {
  let all = 0;
  let lost = 0;
  for (const r of pol.ww.scenario.landRegions) {
    if (pol.legalOwner[r.id] !== id) continue;
    all += r.vp;
    if (pol.land.controller[r.id] !== id) lost += r.vp;
  }
  return all === 0 ? 0 : Math.floor((lost * 100) / all);
}

function groupEvery(pol: WwPolitics, group: string, test: (r: number) => boolean): boolean {
  const g = pol.content.groupIdx.get(group);
  return g !== undefined && g.regions.length > 0 && g.regions.every(test);
}

function evalLeaf(ctx: EffectCtx, c: LeafCondition): boolean {
  const pol = ctx.pol;
  switch (c.kind) {
    case "always": return c.value;
    case "date_ge": return ctx.day >= dayOfDate(c.date);
    case "date_lt": return ctx.day < dayOfDate(c.date);
    case "historical_ai": return pol.mg.config().historicalAi();
    case "gflag": return pol.gflags.has(c.flag);
    case "wt_ge": return pol.wtBp >= c.pct * 100;
    default: break;
  }
  const id = c.who === undefined ? ctx.self : resolveSelector(ctx, c.who);
  if (id === 0) return false;
  if (c.kind === "exists") return pol.living(id);
  if (c.kind === "capitulated") return pol.ww.isCapitulated(id);
  if (!pol.living(id)) return false;
  const p = pol.country(id)!;
  const ce = pol.econ.country(id);
  const other = (s: Parameters<typeof resolveSelector>[1]) => resolveSelector(ctx, s);
  switch (c.kind) {
    case "days_since_flag_ge": {
      const f = p.flags.get(c.flag);
      return f !== undefined && ctx.day - f[0] >= c.days;
    }
    case "tag": return pol.tagOf(id) === c.tag;
    case "is_major": return COUNTRIES[pol.tagOf(id)].major;
    case "is_ai": return !pol.isHuman(id);
    case "is_human": return pol.isHuman(id);
    case "ruling": return p.ruling === IDEOLOGIES.indexOf(c.ideology);
    case "pop_ge": return p.pop[IDEOLOGIES.indexOf(c.ideology)] >= c.pct * 100;
    case "stability_ge": return p.stabBp >= c.pct * 100;
    case "ws_ge": return p.wsBp >= c.pct * 100;
    case "lost_vp_ge": return lostLegalVpPct(pol, id) >= c.pct;
    case "pp_ge": return p.ppMilli >= c.amount * 1000;
    case "army_xp_ge": return (pol.land.country(id)?.armyXpMilli ?? 0) >= c.amount * 1000;
    case "has_spirit": return p.hasSpirit(c.id);
    case "focus_done": {
      const pos = pol.content.treePos.get(c.id);
      const fi = pol.content.focusIdx.get(c.id);
      return pos !== undefined && fi !== undefined && pol.content.focuses[fi].tree === p.tree && p.focusState[pos] !== 0;
    }
    case "focus_in_progress": return p.focus?.id === c.id;
    case "decision_taken": return p.taken.has(c.id);
    case "has_advisor": return p.advisors.includes(c.person);
    case "leader": return p.leader === c.person;
    case "flag": return p.hasFlag(c.flag, ctx.day);
    case "law": return ce?.laws[c.law] === c.value;
    case "law_ge": {
      const ladder = LAW_LADDERS[c.law];
      return ce !== null && ladder.indexOf(ce.laws[c.law]) >= ladder.indexOf(c.value);
    }
    case "has_tech": return ce?.researched.has(c.tech) ?? false;
    case "factories_ge": return ce !== null && ce.totals.civ + ce.totals.mil >= c.count;
    case "divisions_ge": return divisionCount(pol, id) >= c.count;
    case "strength_ratio_ge": {
      const o = other(c.vs);
      return o !== 0 && armyStrength(pol, id) * 1000 >= c.permille * Math.max(1, armyStrength(pol, o));
    }
    case "owns_group": return groupEvery(pol, c.group, (r) => pol.legalOwner[r] === id);
    case "controls_group": return groupEvery(pol, c.group, (r) => pol.land.controller[r] === id);
    case "controls_capital_of": {
      const o = other(c.of);
      const r = o === 0 ? 0 : capitalRegion(pol, o);
      return r > 0 && pol.land.controller[r] === id;
    }
    case "neighbor_of": return pol.econ.countryNeighbours(id).includes(other(c.of));
    case "at_war": return pol.ww.hasWar(id);
    case "at_war_with": return pol.ww.isAtWar(id, other(c.of));
    case "war_days_ge": return pol.econ.warDays(id, ctx.tick) >= c.days;
    case "in_faction": return pol.factionIdx(id) >= 0;
    case "faction_leader": return pol.faction(pol.factionIdx(id))?.leader === id;
    case "same_faction": return pol.sameFaction(id, other(c.of));
    case "faction_ideology": {
      const f = pol.factionIdx(id);
      return f >= 0 && pol.factionIdeology(f) === IDEOLOGIES.indexOf(c.ideology);
    }
    case "has_war_goal_on": return pol.hasWarGoal(id, other(c.of));
    case "justifying_on": return p.justification !== null && p.justification.on === other(c.of);
    case "justified_against": return pol.list().some((x) => x.justification?.on === id);
    case "nap_with": return pol.isAtWarBlockedByNap(id, other(c.of));
    case "guarantees": return pol.guarantees.some((g) => g.by === id && g.to === other(c.of));
    case "guaranteed_by": return pol.guarantees.some((g) => g.to === id && g.by === other(c.of));
    case "has_access_from": return pol.hasAccess(id, other(c.of));
    case "threat_ge": return (pol.threatBp[other(c.of)] ?? 0) >= c.pct * 100;
    case "opinion_ge": return opinionOf(pol, id, other(c.of)) >= c.value;
    default: return false;
  }
}
```

  land.setController(region, to, tick, false);
  const p = land.mg.playerBySmallID(to);
  if (!p.isPlayer()) return;
  for (const t of land.scenario.regionTiles(region)) if (land.mg.ownerID(t) !== to) p.conquer(t);
}
export function evictDivisions(land: WwLand, region: number, owner: number): void {}
export function evictArmies(land: WwLand, owner: number, from: number): void {}

export function removeGeneral(land: WwLand, id: string): void {
  land.ww.politics.removedGenerals.add(id);
  const n = GENERALS.findIndex((g) => g.id === id) + 1;
  if (n === 0) return;
  for (const cl of land.countries()) {
    const g = cl.generals.find((x) => x.id === n);
    if (g === undefined || g.army === 0) continue;
    const a = land.armies.get(g.army);
    if (a !== undefined) {
      a.general = 0;
      land.markArmy(a.id);
    }
    g.army = 0;
  }
}
export function restoreGeneral(land: WwLand, id: string): void {
  land.ww.politics.removedGenerals.delete(id);
}
/** Plan decision 19: P2 dates, not purged, not an advisor right now. */
export function generalAvailable(land: WwLand, def: GeneralDef, day: number): boolean {
  return generalServes(def, day) && !land.ww.politics.generalRemoved(def.id) && !land.ww.politics.advisorHolds(def.id);
}

`HistoryBeats.ts`의 기반 본문(결정 15 — E가 나머지를 더할 때 이 두 행동의 동작을 바꾸지 않는다):

```ts
import { WW_WAR_GOAL_DAYS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/PoliticsTypes";
import { declareWar, joinWar } from "../Diplomacy";
import type { WwPolitics } from "./WwPolitics";

/** Spec §18.2: every beat due by `day`, in table order; only an AI actor acts. */
export function runBeats(pol: WwPolitics, day: number, tick: number): void {
  pol.content.beats.forEach((b, i) => {
    const s = pol.beats[i];
    if (s === undefined || s.done || s.skipped || s.fireDay > day) return;
    s.done = true;
    const a = pol.idOfTag(b.actor);
    if (a === 0 || !pol.living(a) || pol.isHuman(a)) return;
    if (b.act.kind === "declare" || b.act.kind === "join") beatWar(pol, a, b.act.kind, b.act.target, day, tick);
    // Task 3 adds: decision, event, focus, diplo, windowDays, the safety-net skip.
  });
}

/** No penalty on the history path: a pact is dropped first and a goal made on the spot (spec §18.2). */
export function beatWar(pol: WwPolitics, a: number, kind: "declare" | "join", targetTag: CountryTag, day: number, tick: number): void {
  const t = pol.idOfTag(targetTag);
  const ap = pol.player(a);
  const tp = pol.player(t);
  if (t === 0 || ap === null || tp === null || !pol.living(t)) return;
  const lo = Math.min(a, t);
  const hi = Math.max(a, t);
  pol.naps = pol.naps.filter((n) => !(n.a === lo && n.b === hi));
  const c = pol.country(a)!;
  if (!pol.hasWarGoal(a, t)) c.warGoals.push({ on: t, type: "conquer", group: "", untilDay: day + WW_WAR_GOAL_DAYS });
  if (kind === "declare") declareWar(pol.mg, pol.ww, ap, tp, tick);
  else joinWar(pol.mg, pol.ww, ap, tp, tick);
}
```
